- **unversioned-2026-10-01-constellation-the-mesh-discovers-peers-and-refuses-to-call-them**: An install of Ymir had no way to know that other installs of Ymir exist. The mesh was a claim, not a surface: `packages/contracts/src/agent-card.ts` defined the card, `packages/a2a/ratatoskr` served one, and nothing on any seat could READ one. So discovery — the cheapest, safest half of a federation — was simply absent. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-01-constellation-the-mesh-discovers-peers-and-refuses-to-call-them.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-04-one-extension-tree-and-a-root-that-walks-up**: The Pi extensions lived in **two** trees, and `bin/seat/valknut-load.sh` copied the second into `&lt;agent-dir&gt;/extensions/`. Pi loads the global and the project directory and does **not** de-duplicate, so the moment the extensions moved into the repo the deployed copy collided with them and **every extension refused to load**: ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-04-one-extension-tree-and-a-root-that-walks-up.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-04-rule-13-and-its-smoke-test**: The layout investigation produced findings but no **law**. Everything it uncovered — the split source, the stale deploy, the two contradictory descriptions, the collision that seats nobody — was a *fact someone had to remember*. A rule makes the memory unnecessary, and a test makes the rule enforceable. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-04-rule-13-and-its-smoke-test.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-04-the-paths-stop-contradicting-themselves**: The Allfather opened with *"why are these extensions in lib?"* — a question the documentation should have answered in one look and could not, because it answered it two ways. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-04-the-paths-stop-contradicting-themselves.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-04-the-tree-mirrors-the-source**: Rule 13 §3 says a multi-file extension is a **directory with an `index.ts`**, and an extension's own internals live inside it. **The tree did that in no places**, and the reason was measurable rather than stylistic: **`lib/` was imported by extensions that had no relationship to the modules.** ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-04-the-tree-mirrors-the-source.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-06-the-errand-door-reported-every-verdict-wrong**: Two failures, both found while filing plan 71, and both of the same species: **a broken thing failed silently, and the silence was shaped exactly like success.** ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-06-the-errand-door-reported-every-verdict-wrong.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-07-the-doctrine-still-described-the-retired-two-home-design**: **Dated:** 2026-10-07 · **Component:** agents (Pi extension surface) · **Branch:** `docs/pi-extensions-one-home` ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-07-the-doctrine-still-described-the-retired-two-home-design.md))

- **unversioned-2026-10-10-private-leaks-and-the-excalidraw-door-moves-to-uncommon-ports**: A batch of small, related corrections carried in the working tree: ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-10-private-leaks-and-the-excalidraw-door-moves-to-uncommon-ports.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-11-four-of-five-copies-of-the-vault-resolver-returned-the-hoard**: **Cites:** `.pi/extensions/ymirhome.ts:8` — the one copy that learned this on 2026-10-08, and wrote the lesson in its own comment. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-11-four-of-five-copies-of-the-vault-resolver-returned-the-hoard.md))
- **unversioned-2026-10-21-seat-identity-honours-ymir-host**: Under the agnostic law (`AGENTS.md`, `RULES/04`, `RULES/25`) the machine and person names above are read as roles: a machine of the fleet. The entry above is append-only (`RULES/06`), so it stands exactly as it was written and this correction is added, not a rewrite — that is why no name is repeated here. Whether the old lines are ever redacted is the Allfather's decision. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-10-21-seat-identity-honours-ymir-host.md))

- **unversioned-2026-11-14-a-full-origin-is-not-stamped-twice**: The first report `fleet-truth-2` sent carried `--from "agent fleet-truth-2 (pi) · herdr pane w39:p0 · heimdall"`, and `msg_origin` appended the road and the machine to it again, so the fleet log recorded *"agent fleet-truth-2 (pi) · herdr pane w39:p0 · heimdall · herdr pane w39:p0 · heimdall"*. A stamp that repeats itself is not wrong about WHO spoke, but it is noise in the one record the Allfathe ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-11-14-a-full-origin-is-not-stamped-twice.md))
- **unversioned-2026-11-14-the-stamp-proves-its-pane**: Plan 275, the stamp fault found at 04:50: a worker's report arrived stamped *"agent Hœnir (the helm, claude) · herdr pane w39:pT"* when the worker was neither. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-11-14-the-stamp-proves-its-pane.md))
- **unversioned-2026-11-14-the-terminal-act-lost-half-its-work**: The whole-tree gate went red on `main` on the `everything-up` row, three checks at once, all of them in `bin/agents/eindri-acclaim.sh`. The blame is one merge: **#718** (`d20dbbe8`, "the report speaks into the pane") brought its branch's copy of the file in whole, and that copy predated **#695**, **#698** and **#700**. The pane-notify tail was the only thing added; everything the earlier hands had ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-2026-11-14-the-terminal-act-lost-half-its-work.md))
- **unversioned-a-worktree-cannot-corrupt-the-shared-skill-index**: The skill index is **one file in the home** (`$YMIR_HOME/.agents/skills/skills.yaml`), shared by every checkout on the machine. But `skill-index.sh` and `skill-find.sh` resolved the `ships`/`local` shelves from `$ROOT` — whichever checkout was running. Run from a Yggdrasil worktree, that is *the worktree's* shipped skills, so: ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-a-worktree-cannot-corrupt-the-shared-skill-index.md))

- **unversioned-rule-25-gains-the-instance-that-proves-it**: Rule 25 (*agnostic by construction*) was written on 2026-10-18 from that day's wounds. The next day, the rule found its own instance from the other side: the skill index shipped to heimdall with **192 absolute `/home/&lt;seat&gt;/…` paths**, so on the second machine the finder resolved nothing, `--check` read it as incomplete forever, and the home went dirty every session. ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-rule-25-gains-the-instance-that-proves-it.md))

- **unversioned-the-skill-index-is-portable-across-machines**: The skill index **did not work on heimdall**. `bin/agents/skill-index.sh` wrote each skill's `path` as an **absolute** path — `192` of them under `/home/&lt;this-seat&gt;/…` — and the index is committed to the **home repo, which syncs to every machine**. On the second machine every one of those paths was a lie: the finder resolved nothing, `--check` read the index as incomplete forever, and the home wen ([zerwiz/ymir](https://github.com/zerwiz/ymir/blob/HEAD/docs/fixes/agents/unversioned-the-skill-index-is-portable-across-machines.md))

- **uo-conversationalist**: Reply to a player who spoke to the UO character. Give it the [TALK] line verbatim. It gathers game context (state, surroundings, what the character has been doing, the conversation so far), decides whether the message is really aimed at us, replies on the channel it arrived on, and reports what it said. Use when a [TALK] line appears from the speech listener. ([johnpwrs/navrey](https://github.com/johnpwrs/navrey/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/uo-conversationalist.md) · ★18)
- **uo-editor-engineer**: Owns the GUO editor: the Godot EditorPlugin under godot/GUO/addons/guo_editor (asset panels, inspector, later the World tab and its tools), runtime hooks under src/Editor, tools/world and tools/editor_smoke. Use for anything in the UO workbench of docs/editor_plan.md: browsing client assets in the editor, the world viewer/editor, world projects and their export, shard world objects. Hands renderer questions to uo-render-engineer and packet or shard-endpoint questions to uo-network-engineer. ([DatMoshu/GodotUO](https://github.com/DatMoshu/GodotUO/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/uo-editor-engineer.md) · ★6)
- **uo-fileformat-engineer**: Owns reading Ultima Online client data: the .mul/.uop/.idx readers, the asset loaders built on them, the patch layers (verdata, mapdif, stadif), and the runtime decode cache. Use for anything under src/IO or src/Assets, and whenever the client fails to load game data. ([DatMoshu/GodotUO](https://github.com/DatMoshu/GodotUO/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/uo-fileformat-engineer.md) · ★6)
- **uo-multi-architect**: Owns authoring new UO multis (houses, keeps, castles, boats): data-mining the client's existing multis and buildings for layout rules, a multi description format with JSON sidecars, generators and validators, writing multis into a staged data set through tools/uodata_write (ADR-0022), furnishing interiors, and proving them in game on the private shard. Use for anything that builds or changes a multi, or turns a reference (a plan, a sketch, a 3D model) into one. ([DatMoshu/GodotUO](https://github.com/DatMoshu/GodotUO/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/uo-multi-architect.md) · ★6)
- **uo-network-engineer**: Owns the UO network stack: the packet table, login and game-server handshakes, the encryption variants, compression, and packet handlers. Use for anything under src/Network, for shard connection failures, and for desync between client state and server state. ([DatMoshu/GodotUO](https://github.com/DatMoshu/GodotUO/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/uo-network-engineer.md) · ★6)
- **uo-port-strategist**: Owns the ClassicUO to Godot port plan: which subsystem is ported next, in what order, and to what tier. Reads the port audit, keeps docs/port_plan.md and docs/port_status.md honest, and arbitrates when a file resists its assigned tier. Consult before starting any new subsystem, and whenever a port turns out harder than its tier predicted. ([DatMoshu/GodotUO](https://github.com/DatMoshu/GodotUO/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/uo-port-strategist.md) · ★6)
- **uo-render-engineer**: Owns the rewrite tier: reimplementing ClassicUO's FNA renderer, input and audio on Godot 4. Covers isometric tile and sprite drawing, the hue shader, texture atlases, draw ordering, gump rendering, and input mapping. Use for anything under src/Render or src/Input, and for any visual difference from the original client. ([DatMoshu/GodotUO](https://github.com/DatMoshu/GodotUO/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/uo-render-engineer.md) · ★6)
- **up-docs-audit-drift**: Audits repo, wiki, and Notion for drift against live state using session context plus live-state queries. Reports findings only — never auto-fixes. Escalates to the orchestrator when findings exceed 10 or when any fix would require destructive action. ([L3DigitalNet/Claude-Code-Plugins](https://github.com/L3DigitalNet/Claude-Code-Plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/up-docs/agents/up-docs-audit-drift.md) · ★8)
- **up-docs-propagate-notion**: Propagates named session changes into Notion at the strategic/organizational layer. Never performs drift detection. Never edits pages outside the session change summary. Never writes code, configs, or step-by-step procedures. ([L3DigitalNet/Claude-Code-Plugins](https://github.com/L3DigitalNet/Claude-Code-Plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/up-docs/agents/up-docs-propagate-notion.md) · ★8)
- **up-docs-propagate-repo**: Propagates named session changes into repository documentation (README.md, docs/, CLAUDE.md, .claude/rules/). Never performs drift detection beyond its mandatory handoff live-state audit. Never edits anything not in the session change summary, except the mandatory audit and stale-file scan. ([L3DigitalNet/Claude-Code-Plugins](https://github.com/L3DigitalNet/Claude-Code-Plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/up-docs/agents/up-docs-propagate-repo.md) · ★8)
- **up-docs-propagate-wiki**: Propagates named session changes into the llm-wiki knowledge base (remote LXC CT 103, /srv/workspaces/llm-wiki, over SSH) at the implementation-reference layer. Writes status:draft pages under the llm-wiki contract; never self-promotes. Never performs drift detection. Never edits pages outside the session change summary. ([L3DigitalNet/Claude-Code-Plugins](https://github.com/L3DigitalNet/Claude-Code-Plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/up-docs/agents/up-docs-propagate-wiki.md) · ★8)

(3) Teardown: research how shipped apps solve the same problem (Mobbin MCP when available, web otherwise).
